- **cog-mem-validate**: Post-task memory validation — learns durable knowledge and consolidates short-term memories in one call ([zig-whatwg/crane](https://github.com/zig-whatwg/crane/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cog-mem-validate.md) · ★12)

- **cogitator**: Sonnet cruncher-lever warden. Diffs the DSL→cruncher buff extraction before/after a re-authoring pass so cosine-motivated re-phrasing cannot silently drop stackable-buff levers. Use for "check the cruncher levers survived this re-author", "diff coverage for &lt;faction&gt; against the previous state". Prompt must include the touched ability_ids/factions and a before-state reference (committed baseline or a saved coverage snapshot). Returns a single JSON object as final message. - **cogmo-reviewer**: Read-only conformance reviewer for cogmo. Complements the generic bug-hunting /code-review; use before opening a PR or approving a design. ([iskhakovt/cogmo](https://github.com/iskhakovt/cogmo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cogmo-reviewer.md))

([AcKeskin/contexture](https://github.com/AcKeskin/contexture/blob/HEAD/copilot/agents/cold-reader.agent.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cold-reader**: Executes one cold-read batch; assessment only, never edits. ([FiredMosquito831/my-writing-companion](https://github.com/FiredMosquito831/my-writing-companion/blob/HEAD/agents/cold-reader.md) · ★1)
- **cold-reader**: 文脈を遮断した初見の読み手。道具を一切持たず、プロンプトに貼られた本文だけを読む。 ([raiki61/claude-plugins](https://github.com/raiki61/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/convergence-loops/agents/cold-reader.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **cold-reader**: Cold audience for documents others will act on: reads a deliverable with zero conversation context and reports every point where its intended audience must ask, guess, or search. Use proactively before a handoff, runbook, README, or work item leaves the session. A structurally cold read beats a simulated one: spawn it rather than re-reading in-session. ([smithdak/skillsmith](https://github.com/smithdak/skillsmith/blob/HEAD/agents/cold-reader.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **cold-reader**: 冷读测试子代理。扮演一个刚刚读完上一章、对后续剧情一无所知的普通读者，评估当前章节的留存钩子强度、信息密度和阅读体验。在 Phase 2.1 初稿完成后调用。使用 Sonnet 模型。 ([a11003070312/novelops](https://github.com/a11003070312/novelops/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cold-reader.md))
- **cold-reader**: Context-restricted subagent that receives ONLY a rendered deliverable + the audience role (never the plan/diff/rationale) and reads it as that recipient — flagging what the audience can't use and content meant for another reader. Use at define-done when the audience didn't live the session. Output mirrors the artifact's language, default English. ([arthuravilacompass/agent-kit](https://github.com/arthuravilacompass/agent-kit/blob/HEAD/plugins/core/agents/cold-reader.md))
- **cold-reader**: Blind editor critiquing a draft blog post as a first-time reader — no session context. Receives only the draft path and the methodology references, returns a five-section structured critique (takeaway mirror, lost points, session residue, arc assessment, AI-tell instances). Read-only. Used by /blog-post and /post-rewrite so the same context that wrote the draft never judges it alone. ([derio-net/blog-craft](https://github.com/derio-net/blog-craft/blob/HEAD/agents/cold-reader.md))
- **cold-reader**: Reads one document with no context from the conversation that wrote it, answers the questions a real reader would arrive with, and reports what the page failed to answer — ambiguity, contradictions, and the knowledge it assumes you already have. Use it to test a document before shipping it. Reports findings only; never edits the document. ([maxim-uvarov/my-claude-skills](https://github.com/maxim-uvarov/my-claude-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/my-skills/agents/cold-reader.md))
- **cold-reader**: Read a document or a repository as a newcomer with no prior context and report every point where it is ambiguous, assumes missing knowledge, or sends the reader in a circle. Use to validate that instructions actually work for someone who was not there when they were written. ([moritztng/tt-bio-bringup](https://github.com/moritztng/tt-bio-bringup/blob/HEAD/agents/cold-reader.md))
- **cold-reader-prompt**: Fill every `{slot}` before dispatch. ([innovaitionpartners/workslop-detector](https://github.com/innovaitionpartners/workslop-detector/blob/HEAD/skills/workslop-detector/agents/cold-reader-prompt.md))
- **cold-reading-comparative**: Cold reading at the comparative position. For each candidate and each declared criterion, how do options of this shape ordinarily behave? Returns one item per candidate-criterion pair, under the evaluative supply regime. ([intentdriven/abcd](https://github.com/intentdriven/abcd/blob/HEAD/agents/cold-reading-comparative.md) · ★3)
- **cold-reading-detection**: Cold reading at the detection position. Where is the shipped tree in tension with the claim record? Returns tensions, each with the constraint in play and why it is a tension, under the registrative supply regime. ([intentdriven/abcd](https://github.com/intentdriven/abcd/blob/HEAD/agents/cold-reading-detection.md) · ★3)
- **cold-reading-entailment**: Cold reading at the entailment position. What does this design commit to, by being the kind of thing it is, that its articulation does not state? Returns surfaced claims, each with its claim type and what implies it, under the explicative supply regime. ([intentdriven/abcd](https://github.com/intentdriven/abcd/blob/HEAD/agents/cold-reading-entailment.md) · ★3)
- **cold-reading-widening**: Cold reading at the widening position. Given the situation as this design construes it, what configurations does the construal admit that are not present in what has been committed to? Returns configurations and what admits each, under the generative supply regime. ([intentdriven/abcd](https://github.com/intentdriven/abcd/blob/HEAD/agents/cold-reading-widening.md) · ★3)
- **cold-reading-widening**: ABCD-EVAL-SENTINEL-DEFINITION: the reading definition, which is the instrument. ([intentdriven/abcd](https://github.com/intentdriven/abcd/blob/HEAD/evals/testdata/cold-reading/baseline/agents/cold-reading-widening.md) · ★3)
- **cold-reviewer**: Zero-context code reviewer. Reviews changes cold - no spec, no project knowledge, no domain context. Catches what familiarity blinds you to. - **cold-solver**: Blind checker for Economics Academy content. - **comment-ablation-auditor**: Adversarial reviewer of one comment-ablation diff. Reads the removed `#` blocks and the surviving ones in an assigned file group, re-derives each deleted fact's recoverability from the code alone, and reports either CLEAN-CUT (lossless) or DEEP-CUT (a fact the code cannot supply, with the keep class it should have survived under) — plus EARNED or UNEARNED for every surviving comment. Read-only: its tool set has no edit capability. Never reads the ablation's own reports, never invokes kata, never fixes anything. One-off, scoped to the ablation campaign's review wave. - **comment-ablator**: Comment-ablation executor for one assignment of files. Reads the comment-ablation skill first, then deletes `#` comments the code already supplies, keeping only the four narrow classes, reporting contradictions and work orders instead of fixing them, and proving every file code-inert with the skill's gate. Reads a baseline before editing so a gate failure is attributable to its own change. One-off, scoped to the ablation campaign. Never touches string literals, docstrings, non-comment lines, or AGENTS.md maps; never commits. ([taylorsatula/mira](https://github.com/taylorsatula/mira/blob/HEAD/.pi/agents/comment-ablator.md) · ★484)

- **commit-writer**: Drafts a Conventional Commits message for the currently staged changes and creates the commit. Reads the modified files in full (not just the diff hunks) before classifying — that step is the difference between `feat: update auth.js` and `fix(auth): prevent JWT alg=none bypass`. Use after staging changes when you want classification + formatting without burning reasoning-model tokens. Stops if intent is ambiguous or the diff is too large — does not guess. ([felixhennequin-gif/claude-code-config-template](https://github.com/felixhennequin-gif/claude-code-config-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/commit-writer.md) · 2 copies)
